Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) safety systems combine character shaping (e.g., Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback [RLHF], Constitutional AI), which modifies behavioral distributions at training time, with rule enforcement (e.g., output filters, safety classifiers), which blocks harmful outputs at inference time, yet little formal analysis exists on how their optimal balance should change as deployment scales increase. We introduce a stylized comparative-statics model that parameterizes safety design as a resource allocation alpha in [0,1] between these two approaches, incorporating scale-dependent filter degradation, common-mode failures, and character fragility -- the risk that shaped behavior degrades or collapses under novel conditions. Under a multiplicative Pareto damage model, we derive closed-form expected harm and supplement it with tail-risk (CVaR) analysis via Monte Carlo simulation. Across three scenarios (optimistic, moderate, pessimistic), the optimal alpha* is interior or at the rules-only boundary and shifts weakly toward character shaping as deployment scale T grows, from negligible (Delta alpha* = +0.01) to pronounced (Delta alpha* = +0.21) depending on scenario. The dominant parameter is the baseline character fragility rate p^(0)_frag, which shifts alpha* by 0.50 across its range -- far exceeding the effect of tail severity, filter quality, or common-mode failure probability. CVaR and expected-harm optima converge at large T. These results suggest that safety architecture decisions depend less on deployment scale per se than on the reliability of character shaping under distributional shift.

</details>


### [165] [TopoIntent: Compiling Security Intent into Executable, Compliance-Checked Network Topologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13389)
*Xiaokang Qu,Jianliang Ma,Zao Fan,Tianshu Chu,Tianlong Fan,Linyuan Lü*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TopoIntent是一个将安全意图编译为可执行、合规校验的网络拓扑的系统。它通过schema契约约束生成、基于稠密向量检索参考架构模板、采用分阶段融合实现意图-模板对齐和安全补全，生成的拓扑对照CIS Controls v8.1.2进行校验，未解析案例标记人工复核，结构缺口通过保持schema的增量编辑修复，最终导出为带iptables ACL的Mininet脚本。由于该任务无公开基准，作者基于参考安全架构图构建了评估集，实验表明增量修复将拓扑可见CIS满足率从0.78提升至1.00，一轮反馈后将ACL策略通过率从0.78提升至0.88。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业安全拓扑设计需要将业务意图、合规要求和风险假设转化为分区、边界设备、跨区路径和访问控制策略。现有NetOps自动化工具主要在拓扑设计完成后才介入，对从非结构化自然语言需求生成结构化安全拓扑的支持十分有限。

Method: TopoIntent采用以下方法：(1) 使用schema契约约束生成过程；(2) 通过稠密向量搜索从精选模板库检索参考架构；(3) 应用分阶段融合实现意图-模板对齐与安全补全；(4) 生成的拓扑对照CIS Controls v8.1.2中拓扑层可见的防护项进行校验，未解决场景标记为人工复核；(5) 通过保持schema的增量编辑（additive schema-preserving edits）修复结构缺口；(6) 导出为带内核级iptables ACL的Mininet脚本以支持可达性与allow/deny测试。

Result: 在保留的7个模板和14个金融/政府场景意图的测试集上，增量修复将拓扑可见CIS满足率从0.78提升至1.00，平均不到1.5轮完成；一轮反馈后ACL策略通过率从0.78提升至0.88。

Conclusion: TopoIntent验证了从自然语言安全意图生成合规、可执行网络拓扑的可行性，通过模板检索、schema约束生成、增量修复与反馈闭环，能够在少轮迭代内达到完全满足拓扑层CIS防护要求的水平，为需求到拓扑的自动化设计提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Enterprise security topology design requires translating business intent, regulatory requirements, and risk assumptions into zones, boundary devices, inter-zone paths, and access-control policies. Existing NetOps automation tools mainly operate after this design is fixed, providing limited support for generating structured security topologies from underspecified natural-language requirements. We present TopoIntent, a system that compiles security intent into executable, compliance-checked network topologies. It uses a schema contract to constrain generation, retrieves reference architectures from a curated template library via dense-vector search, and applies staged fusion for intent-template alignment and security completion. The generated topology is checked against CIS Controls v8.1.2 safeguards visible at the topology layer, while unresolved cases are marked for manual review. Structural gaps are repaired through additive schema-preserving edits. The final topology is exported to Mininet scripts with kernel-level iptables ACLs, enabling executable reachability and allow/deny tests. Because no public benchmark exists for this requirement-to-topology task, we construct an evaluation set from reference security architecture diagrams. The retrieval set contains 22 templates and 44 synthetic intents across five scenarios, while the held-out set contains 7 templates and 14 intents from finance and government scenarios excluded from retrieval. On the held-out set, additive repair improves topology-visible CIS satisfaction from 0.78 to 1.00 in fewer than 1.5 rounds on average, and one feedback round raises the post-ACL policy pass rate from 0.78 to 0.88.

</details>


### [166] [Jointly Predicting Courses and Grades Using a Transformer-Based Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13409)
*Paul Savala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE（Transformer for Academic Course-grade Estimation），一种用于学习分析中课程成绩预测的新型神经架构。与现有将学生学业史视为简单序列的模型不同，TRACE以学期为单位对课程进行编码以捕捉课程并修（concurrency）效应，并联合预测学生下学期的课程集合及相应成绩。在十年机构数据上训练后，与仅预测成绩的相同架构相比，平均绝对误差降低近50%，同时优于LSTM序列模型和GNN方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习分析中的预测模型将学生学业历史视为简单序列，忽略了同一学期内多门课程并修的现实情况。这种简化可能导致对课业负担重或课程挑战大的学生的成绩预测不准确。作者旨在解决这一局限，通过联合建模课程选择与成绩预测来提升预测质量。

Method: 提出TRACE模型，采用Transformer架构，以学期为单位编码课程以捕捉课程并修效应。模型联合预测两件事：(1) 学生下个学期将修读的课程集合；(2) 这些课程的对应成绩。为此设计了一种新颖的损失函数，将课程集合预测与成绩预测相结合。模型还可自然融入学生属性数据，支持通过重新训练和校准部署到新机构。

Result: 在十年机构数据上，联合预测模型相比仅预测成绩的相同架构，平均绝对误差降低近50%。模型还优于基于LSTM的序列模型和基于图神经网络的方法。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了现代神经架构在创建可解释的教育预测模型方面的价值，强调了"预测课程修读情况"这一关键训练技巧的重要性。模型可通过重新训练和校准适应新机构，并可集成到高等教育机构的早期检测系统中，用于识别可能存在学业风险的学生。

Abstract: Existing predictive models in learning analytics often treat student academic history as a simple sequence, overlooking the concurrent nature of courses taken within a semester. This simplification can lead to inaccurate performance predictions, particularly for students with heavy or challenging course loads. This paper introduces a TRansformer for Academic Course-grade Estimation (TRACE) that addresses this limitation by jointly predicting both the set of courses a student will take and their corresponding grades for an upcoming semester. Our approach encodes courses on a per-semester basis to capture the effects of course concurrency and utilizes a novel loss function combining course-set prediction with grade prediction. We demonstrate that predicting courses taken in addition to the grades in those courses leads to significant improvements in prediction quality. Trained on ten years of institutional data, our joint prediction model reduces mean absolute error by nearly 50% compared to an identical architecture that predicts grades alone. The model also outperforms traditional LSTM-based sequential models, as well as graph neural network-based approaches, and offers natural ways to incorporate student attribute data. This work demonstrates the utility of modern neural architectures for creating interpretable models that can be adapted to new institutions via retraining and recalibration, as well as the importance of key techniques, such as predicting courses taken during training. We discuss how this model could be incorporated into early detection systems at institutions of higher education.

</details>


### [167] [Who Speaks Matters: Authority-Aware Multi-View RAG over Italian Parliamentary Proceedings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13410)
*Mirko Tritella,Riccardo Pozzi,Matteo Palmonari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ParliamentRAG，一个针对意大利众议院议会记录的检索增强生成系统，核心贡献是主题相关的发言人权威模型，联合解决了议会文本RAG中常见的三大风险：高频发言者主导、无法按主题专业知识加权、引用错误归因。系统在15个政策主题上与Google NotebookLM对比，在政治团体覆盖率、引用忠实度和来源相关维度上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 议会程序是民主协商的主要记录，但其体量和碎片化使公民、记者和研究者难以实现多视角访问。将RAG应用于议会原始记录会引入三个具体风险：最常见发言者的主导地位、无法根据主题专业知识对发言者加权、以及在政治敏感文本中的引用错误归属。这些风险阻碍了公平、准确的多视角信息获取。

Method: 提出ParliamentRAG系统，核心是主题相关的权威模型（topic-dependent authority model），该模型根据当前查询估计每位发言者的权威度，融合职业、教育背景、先前发言等可解释组件。系统流程为：根据用户查询检索相关发言片段，识别跨党派的主题相关专家，生成综合各视角的摘要并附支持性引文。评估采用两级协议，结合自动指标和六位领域专家的盲法A/B人工评估，与Google NotebookLM在15个政策主题上对比。

Result: ParliamentRAG在政治团体覆盖度上更高（0.97 vs. 0.95），引用忠实度达到完美（1.00 vs. 0.95），且在来源相关维度上获得更强的专家偏好；而NotebookLM在散文/文风相关维度上表现更强。

Conclusion: ParliamentRAG通过主题相关的权威模型有效缓解了议会RAG中的三大风险，在来源准确性和多视角覆盖上优于通用RAG工具，但通用工具在生成文本的散文质量方面仍有优势，提示未来工作可结合两者的优点。

Abstract: Parliamentary proceedings are a primary record of democratic deliberation, yet their volume and fragmentation make multi-perspective access difficult for citizens, journalists, and researchers. Applying Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) to parliamentary transcripts introduces three specific risks: dominance of the most frequent speakers, inability to weight speakers according to topical expertise, and citation misattribution in politically sensitive text. We present ParliamentRAG, a RAG system for the Italian Chamber of Deputies that addresses these risks jointly. Its core contribution is a topic-dependent authority model that estimates each speaker's authority as a function of the current query, combining interpretable components such as profession, education, and previous interventions. Given a user query, the system retrieves relevant speech chunks, identifies topic-relevant experts across parliamentary groups, and generates a summary synthesizing their perspectives, accompanied by supporting quotations. ParliamentRAG is evaluated against Google NotebookLM on 15 policy topics via a two-level protocol combining automated metrics and blind A/B human evaluation by six domain experts. The system achieves higher coverage across political groups (0.97 vs. 0.95), perfect quotation faithfulness (1.00 vs. 0.95), and stronger expert preferences on source-related dimensions, while NotebookLM remains stronger on prose-oriented dimensions.

</details>


### [168] [Beyond Final Scores: A Systematic Evaluation of Agents for Long-Horizon AI Research and Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13417)
*Yiwei Li,Wanli Yang,Hexiang Tan,Xiangzhou Huang,Zhengyu Chen,Ziran Li,Borun Chen,Shanglin Lei,Huaisheng Zhu,Hao Tian,Fei Sun,Xunliang Cai,Jingang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个系统性评估框架，用于超越最终分数来评估七个前沿模型在36个长时程任务上的自主智能体能力。通过基于规则的过程指标（Solution Framing、Execution、Feedback Control）和受控对比分析经验重用，发现当前智能体更像"工程优化器"而非"全自主研究者"：能制定和实施实用方案，但跨运行表现波动大、最强方案多为已有技术组合、真正的方法创新罕见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统评估只关注最终分数，无法揭示智能体在长时程实验过程中（1）进展在何处获得或丢失，（2）积累的经验是否有助于后续决策。为理解自主智能体当前的真实能力状态，需要更细粒度的过程性评估框架。

Method: 构建一个系统化评估框架：（1）使用基于规则（rule-based）的指标刻画运行内行为，分为Solution Framing（方案构建）、Execution（执行）、Feedback Control（反馈控制）三个维度；（2）通过受控对比评估任务内与跨任务的经验重用（experience reuse）。对7个前沿模型在36个长时程任务上进行评估。

Result: （1）当前智能体更接近工程优化器而非全自主研究者：能形成并实施实用方案；（2）跨运行表现存在显著波动；（3）最强方案主要依赖对既有技术的适配与组合，方法学上的新颖性罕见；（4）相同最终结果背后可能存在不同的过程瓶颈（process bottlenecks）；（5）经验重用既能帮助也可能误导后续决策；（6）实验台设计（harness design）影响表现稳定性。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了影响智能体表现的多个因素（过程瓶颈、经验重用的双刃剑效应、实验台设计），为模型训练、推理时策略、经验管理与实验台设计提供了具体改进方向。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are increasingly capable of improving models, systems, and other technical artifacts through long-horizon experimentation. To understand the current state of this capability, however, evaluation must go beyond final scores, which neither reveal where progress is gained or lost nor indicate whether accumulated experience improves later decisions. We therefore present a systematic evaluation of seven frontier models on 36 long-horizon tasks based on a new framework that uses rule-based metrics to characterize within-run behavior through Solution Framing, Execution, and Feedback Control and controlled comparisons to assess experience reuse within and across tasks. The results show that current agents operate more like engineering optimizers than fully autonomous researchers: they can formulate and implement practical solutions, but their performance varies substantially across runs, their strongest solutions mainly adapt or combine established techniques, and genuine methodological novelty remains rare. Detailed analysis reveals that observed performance is shaped by multiple factors, including distinct process bottlenecks behind similar final outcomes, experience reuse that can help or mislead subsequent decisions, and harness designs that affect performance stability. These findings suggest concrete directions for improving model training, inference-time strategies, experience management, and harness design.

</details>


### [169] [Enhancing Virtual Agents through SLMs and Edge-Computing: An Exploratory Evaluation of Think and Memory Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13420)
*Aimilios Hadjiliasi,Louis Nisiotis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨使用小型语言模型（SLMs）在边缘计算环境中支持认知具身智能体架构（CEAA）中的"Think"和"Memory"核心组件，并在NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX上通过Qwen2.5模型实现了边缘虚拟智能体网关系统，实验验证了路由准确性、内存读取性能和延迟表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能虚拟智能体需在复杂的虚拟世界和元宇宙中持续、自适应地运行，但实现认知能力在概念和技术上具有挑战性。鉴于边缘计算和生成式AI语言模型的进展，本文探索利用小型语言模型在边缘端支持CEAA中的关键认知组件，以实现高效且情境化的认知运行。

Method: 基于CEAA框架，开发了一个边缘端虚拟智能体网关系统，聚焦于"Think"和"Memory"两个核心过程的实现。系统在NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX上使用不同尺寸的Qwen2.5模型进行部署，通过仿真实验评估服务请求处理、记忆驱动对话、路由准确率、记忆读取性能和延迟等指标。

Result: 仿真实验验证了SLM驱动的原型智能体系统能够部分实现CEAA中的选定过程，在路由准确性、记忆读取性能和延迟方面表现出可行性与有效性，证明了认知"大脑"可以在边缘设备上高效运行。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，结合边缘计算与小型语言模型能够为沉浸式虚拟世界中的具身智能体提供高效、情境化的认知支持，为CEAA框架的边缘化部署和元宇宙智能体开发提供了实践路径。

Abstract: Embodied intelligent virtual agents are expected to operate as persistent, adaptive, and context-aware entities within complex virtual and Metaverse worlds. However, implementing cognitively capable agents in such environments is conceptually and technologically challenging. Among a range of blueprints and development approaches, the Cognitive Embodied Agent Architecture (CEAA) has been developed as an implementation-oriented framework for architecting components of perception, memory, reasoning, planning, and embodied action. Considering the recent advances in edge computing and generative AI language models, this paper explores the use of Small Language Models (SLMs) to support edge-based operation of selected CEAA components, focusing on "Think" and "Memory" as processes central to cognitive orchestration and persistence of virtual agents in interactive virtual worlds. An edge-based virtual agent gateway system was developed and evaluated on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX using Qwen2.5 models of different sizes, exploring the system's capability to process service requests and handle memory-driven conversations. A series of simulation experiments evaluated routing accuracy, memory-read performance, and latency, demonstrating an SLM-driven prototype agent system that partially implements selected CEAA processes to support the development of embodied agents whose cognitive "brain" can operate efficiently and contextually for interactive experiences in immersive virtual worlds.

</details>


### [170] [RAIL: An Automatic Classifier of the Artificial Intelligence Readiness Level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13428)
*Juan Irving Vasquez,Juan Terven,Laura-Ivoone Garay-Jimenez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文统一了三种AI技术成熟度评估框架，提出九级统一AI就绪水平（AIRL）量表及其可自动判定的规则，并设计了基于独立LLM专家小组的分类器RAIL，实现对自然语言描述的研究工作进行自动化、一致且不虚高的成熟度评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能技术成熟度的评估对投资决策、项目管理和政策监测至关重要，但现有成熟度框架（如AI化的TRL、ML-TRL、AI/数据就绪度模型）彼此异构、缺乏针对AI的门控标准、依赖内部过程产物或量纲难以直接比较，且难以自动化应用。

Method: 提出统一AI就绪水平（AIRL）：一个九级有序量表，基于环境证据阶梯构建，辅以维度上限（涵盖规格、数据存在性、数据质量、数据合法性、专家知识、算法成熟度）、通用性锚定规则和明确的赋值规范，使就绪水平可仅从自然语言描述即可判定。在此基础上提出RAIL分类器：由一个证据代理和六个独立维度代理（各为窄范围职责的LLM）组成专家小组，通过确定性最小规则聚合裁决，首席专家在非对称权威下复核，仅可确认或下调建议而不可高于维度上限。

Result: 该方法在多个研究工作分析中进行了测试，结果显示评估一致性好，且避免了单体LLM分类器导致的过度估计问题。

Conclusion: AIRL和RAIL提供了一种可自动化、可解释、评估保守的AI技术成熟度评估方案，统一了现有异构框架，并通过多独立专家的设计与确定性聚合规则抑制了过拟合与虚高估计。

Abstract: Assessing the maturity of artificial intelligence technologies is essential for investment decisions, project management, and policy monitoring, yet the available readiness frameworks are heterogeneous and difficult to apply automatically: the adaptation of Technology Readiness Levels to AI lacks AI-specific gating criteria, the Machine Learning Technology Readiness Levels presuppose access to internal process artifacts, and AI/data readiness dimension models employ scales that resist direct comparison. This paper makes two contributions. First, we unify these three frameworks into the Unified AI Readiness Level (AIRL), a nine-level ordinal scale built on an environmental evidence ladder and complemented by dimensional caps (covering specification, data existence, data quality, data legality, expert knowledge, and algorithmic maturity) together with a generality-anchoring rule and explicit assignment disciplines, so that a readiness level becomes decidable from a natural-language description of the work alone. Second, we propose RAIL (Readiness Assessment via Independent LLM-experts), a panel-of-experts classifier that operationalizes the scale: one evidence agent and six independent dimension agents, each a large language model with a narrowly scoped mandate, deliver verdicts that a deterministic minimum rule aggregates and a chief expert reviews under asymmetric authority, confirming or lowering the panel's recommendation but never raising it above the caps. The method was tested in the analysis of several research works showing consistency and avoiding overestimation from monolithic LLM classifiers.

</details>


### [171] [Academic League of Artificial Intelligence - An Integrative Perspective of Teaching, Research, and Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13447)
*Alison R. Panisson,Maria Eduarda W. M. Vianna,Italo Firmino da Silva,Heitor Henrique da Silva,Rafaela Fernandes Savaris,Bernardo Pandolfi Costa,Martin Augusto Gagliotti Vigil,Jim Lau,Agenor Hentz,Andréa Sabedra Bordin,Alexandre Leopoldo Gonçalves,Roberto Rodrigues-Filho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了巴西圣卡塔琳娜联邦大学人工智能学术联盟（LIA）的组织框架，该框架通过学生为中心、项目驱动的模式整合教学、研究和大学拓展三大支柱，融合民主治理、协作学习和动态项目管理，并通过竞赛团队、学习小组、公开讲座、知识库和具有社会影响力的人工智能应用等项目验证了其可行性和可复制性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术联盟已成为促进课外教育和加强大学与社会融合的重要机制，但缺乏一个灵活、可复制的组织框架来系统性地整合教学、研究与大学拓展三大支柱，特别是在工程和计算教育领域。

Method: 提出一个基于学生中心、项目驱动方法的组织框架，结合民主治理、协作学习和动态项目组织，并在UFSC的LIA联盟中实施。通过竞赛团队、学习小组、公开讲座、知识库和人工智能社会应用等多个代表性项目展示框架的落地效果。

Result: 该框架成功支撑了多样化的教育、科研和拓展活动，促进了学生领导力、科学产出、社区参与和知识保存，表明该模型在工程和计算教育中具有灵活性和可复制性。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架为学术联盟及类似学生组织整合大学三大支柱提供了实用指导，是一个灵活且可复制的模型。

Abstract: Academic leagues have become important mechanisms for promoting extracurricular education and strengthening the integration between universities and society. This paper presents the organizational framework adopted by the Academic League of Artificial Intelligence (LIA) at the Federal University of Santa Catarina (UFSC), designed to integrate teaching, research, and university extension through a student-centered, project-based approach. The framework combines democratic governance, collaborative learning, and dynamic project organization to foster both technical and transversal competencies. The framework is illustrated through representative initiatives, including competition teams, study groups, open lectures, knowledge repositories, and AI-powered applications with social impact. These projects demonstrate how diverse educational, scientific, and extension activities can be developed within a common organizational structure while promoting leadership, scientific production, community engagement, and knowledge preservation. The reported experience indicates that the proposed framework provides a flexible and replicable model for integrating the three university pillars into engineering and computing education, offering practical guidance for academic leagues and similar student organizations.

</details>


### [172] [A Unifying Perspective on Causal World Models: From Observations to Representations to Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13456)
*Avinash Kori,Fabrizio Russo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从因果视角系统研究世界模型，提出因果世界模型（CWM）的形式化定义，强调世界模型应超越生成能力，捕获实体属性、实体间及实体-环境交互等因果结构，并将其与因果表征学习、对象中心学习、因果发现、结构因果模型和基于模型的决策制定等已有工作联系起来，同时讨论了可辨识性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型被视为智能体超越训练分布进行预测、规划和行动的基础，但现有世界模型主要关注生成能力，未充分捕获支撑环境动态的因果结构和关系（如实体属性、实体间及实体-环境交互）。作者认为有用的世界模型必须超越生成能力，因而需要从因果视角重新形式化世界模型。

Method: 从因果视角在多层次抽象上研究世界模型（从感知观测到概念表征），提供因果世界模型（CWM）的形式化定义，将其与因果表征学习、对象中心学习、因果发现、结构因果模型和基于模型的决策制定等已有工作建立联系，并从可辨识性角度分析世界模型各组件何时可被数据恢复。

Result: 提出了Causal World Models（CWMs）的形式化框架，将世界模型锚定在支持因果推理和知情决策的表征与结构上，并阐明了与现有多个研究方向的关系及可辨识性条件。

Conclusion: 世界模型应被理解为不仅具有生成能力，还应捕获决定和解释系统动态的因果结构（实体属性及交互），这一框架为未来构建支持因果推理和明智决策的智能体提供了理论基础。

Abstract: World Models (WM) are increasingly seen as a foundation for intelligent agents that can predict, plan, and act beyond their training distribution. In this paper, we study WMs from a causal perspective across multiple levels of abstraction, ranging from perceptual observations to building a conceptual representation of the structure governing the environment dynamics. We argue that useful WMs must go beyond generative capabilities alone: they should also capture entity properties, entity-to-entity interactions, and entity-to-environment interactions that determine and explain the dynamics of a system. We provide a formal definition of Causal WMs (CWMs) grounded in the tasks they are intended to support, connecting world modelling with existing work in causal representation learning, object-centric learning, causal discovery, structural causal models, and model-based decision-making. Finally, we relate CWMs to the literature on identifiability, clarifying when the components of a WM can be recovered from data and up to which equivalence. With this, we ground WMs in representations and structures that support causal reasoning and informed decision-making.

</details>


### [173] [MARC v1: An Open-Source Multi-Agent Framework for Clinical AI Reasoning and Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13476)
*Saisha Shetty,Satvik Tripathi,Austin Lin,Colin Zhao,Theodore Kim,Don Enwerem,Jacinta Arnold,Shahriar Faghani,Tessa S Cook*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MARC（多智能体推理与协调），一个开源的确定性多智能体编排框架，用于临床推理任务。MARC通过角色专业化智能体（抽取、推理、答案生成和评估）替代单一LLM提示，支持显式上下文传递、可追溯的中间输出，并引入Decomposer模块从自然语言描述自动生成任务特定智能体提示，无需手动提示工程。框架支持API和本地CPU部署，完全YAML可配置，模型无关且对临床专家友好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的临床推理任务通常依赖于单一的LLM提示（monolithic prompting），这种方法缺乏可解释性、难以定位失败阶段，且需要大量手动提示工程。临床领域需要一个可解释、可追溯、对领域专家友好的多智能体框架来替代黑盒式的单一模型推理。

Method: MARC框架采用确定性多智能体编排，包含四个角色专业化智能体：抽取（extraction）、推理（reasoning）、答案生成（answer generation）和评估（evaluation）。智能体之间通过显式上下文传递和可追溯中间输出实现阶段级失败归因。同时引入Decomposer模块，可从纯文本描述自动生成任务特定的智能体提示，消除手动提示工程。框架支持API和本地CPU部署，并通过YAML完全配置。

Result: 框架提供了完全开源实现，支持模型无关的多后端部署（API和本地CPU），全YAML可配置无需代码修改，使得没有编程背景的临床领域专家也可以使用。

Conclusion: MARC通过将单调LLM提示替换为确定性的多智能体协调框架，提升了临床推理的可解释性和可追溯性，并降低了临床专家使用LLM技术的门槛。

Abstract: We present Multi-Agent Reasoning and Coordination (MARC), an open-source framework that replaces monolithic LLM prompting with deterministic multi-agent orchestration for clinical reasoning. MARC coordinates role-specialized agents for extraction, reasoning, answer generation, and evaluation, with explicit context passing and traceable intermediate outputs, enabling stage-wise failure attribution. We additionally introduce a Decomposer module that generates task-specific agent prompts from a plain-language description, eliminating manual prompt engineering. The framework supports both API-based and local CPU-compatible deployments and is entirely configurable via YAML, without code modifications. MARC is designed to be model-agnostic, interpretable, and accessible to clinical domain experts without programming expertise. The full framework is available at https://github.com/Penn-RAIL/MARC-v1.

</details>


### [174] [AlayaWorld: Interactive Long-Horizon World Modeling - Full Technical Report (v1.1)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13492)
*AlayaWorld Team,Kaipeng Zhang,Chuanhao Li,Yifan Zhan,Yongtao Ge,Yuanyang Yin,Jiaming Tan,Kang He,Liaoyuan Fan,Mingliang Zhai,Ruicong Liu,Xiaojie Xu,Xuangeng Chu,Zhen Li,Zhengyuan Lin,Zhixiang Wang,Zian Meng,Zihui Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AlayaWorld的改进版本，在不改变骨干架构、分块自回归生成方案和训练数据的前提下，重新设计了条件信号的表示与集成方式，核心原则是让条件信号与生成内容在潜空间表示和时间结构上尽可能一致，具体通过流式3D点缓存渲染器替换深度扭曲空间记忆，并统一条件编码到因果VAE潜空间等六项修改实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上一版本的AlayaWorld中，条件信号与生成内容在潜空间表示和时间结构上存在不一致，导致条件信息不能充分引导视频生成。作者希望条件信号能更紧密地与生成内容对齐，从而提升生成质量与可控性。

Method: 提出六项关键修改：(1) 用运动感知的潜条件替换静态帧图像条件；(2) 将重渲染的空间记忆因果编码为连续序列；(3) 在像素空间对齐时间记忆窗口；(4) 采用硬记忆丢弃（移除记忆token而非置零）；(5) 统一训练与推理阶段的VAE编解码协议；(6) 移除相机AdaLN分支，视点控制完全由重渲染空间条件提供。整体结构包含流式3D点缓存渲染器作为空间记忆，条件信息在因果VAE潜空间中以与生成视频一致的时间统计特性进行编码。

Result: 通过将条件信号与生成内容在潜表示与时间结构上高度对齐，改善了模型的视频生成表现与条件可控性，同时保持了原有骨干架构和自回归生成方案的一致性。

Conclusion: 条件信号与生成内容在潜空间和时间结构上的对齐是提升视频生成条件控制质量的关键，本版本通过重新设计条件表示与集成方式（而非依赖更大的模型或更多数据）实现了有效改进。

Abstract: This report presents an improved version of AlayaWorld. While the backbone architecture, chunk-wise autoregressive generation scheme, and training data remain unchanged from the previous release, we substantially revise how conditioning signals are represented and integrated into the model. The new design is guided by a simple principle: conditioning signals should match the generated content as closely as possible in both latent representation and temporal structure. To this end, we make two major changes. First, we replace the previous depth-warping-based spatial memory with a streaming 3D point-cache renderer. Second, we redesign the conditioning pipeline so that visual conditions are encoded in the same causal-VAE latent space, with temporal statistics consistent with those of the generated video. Concretely, the new version introduces six modifications: (1) replacing static-frame image conditioning with motion-aware latent conditioning; (2) causally encoding re-rendered spatial memory as a continuous sequence; (3) aligning the temporal-memory window in pixel space; (4) adopting hard memory dropout that removes memory tokens rather than zeroing them; (5) unifying the VAE encoding and decoding protocol across training and inference; and (6) removing the camera AdaLN branch, such that viewpoint control is provided entirely through the re-rendered spatial condition.

</details>


### [175] [QuoteBench: How Matched Scores Can Hide Command-Path Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13547)
*Shangao Li,Yao Zhang,Volker Tresp,Yuanyuan Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出QuoteBench，一个用于区分LLM编码代理中命令生成错误与执行传输层引入错误的基准。通过56个一次性任务、14个事件衍生场景，在生成契约与执行传输的交接处人为添加一个故意未转义的解析器，定量测量边界适应性。结果显示通过额外解析器重放相同回复使成功率降低55.4至73.2个百分点，而披露边界信息可使六个配置恢复30.4至60.7个百分点。关键发现是匹配分数会掩盖严重的生成损伤与补偿，且部署配置会重新排列模型排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的匹配执行分数（matched execution scores）无法区分命令生成错误与生成之后由传输层（序列化、包装、重新解析）引入的失败。这种混淆使得评估结果无法准确反映模型的真实生成能力，需要一种方法定量测量生成与执行传输之间的边界。

Method: QuoteBench包含56个一次性任务，来自14个事件衍生（incident-derived）类别。方法核心是在生成契约与执行传输的交接处添加一个故意未转义的解析器，通过精确最终状态验证（exact final-state validation）测量这个边界。在插值点进行转义可以复现每个重放回复的原始路径结果，因此只有在披露边界条件后模型的恢复才来自生成改变。跨八个相同窗口配置进行重放测试，比较加解析器前后的成功率差异。

Result: 通过额外解析器重放相同回复会使八种配置的成功率下降55.4至73.2个百分点；披露边界信息后，六个配置恢复30.4至60.7个百分点，另外两个配置恢复为零或略为负值。原始生成本身在前沿几乎饱和，边界适应性是区分模型的关键。GPT-5.6-sol的匹配差距为-3.6个百分点，但掩盖了-64.3个百分点的损伤和+60.7个百分点的补偿。在26对可比较的模型对比中有一个明确的重排，另四个仅凭单任务边缘决定。

Conclusion: 对命令发布代理的评估应当报告模型配置、生成契约、执行路径、操作点和最终状态验证器，而不能将匹配分数视为模型的内在属性。部署配置会显著影响模型排名，评价体系需要对此进行显式披露。

Abstract: LLM coding agents issue Bash commands through interfaces that may serialize, wrap, and reparse model output. Matched execution scores alone cannot distinguish command-generation errors from failures introduced after generation. QuoteBench measures this boundary with exact final-state validation on 56 one-shot tasks from 14 incident-derived families, crossing the generation contract with the execution transport around one deliberately unescaped added parser. Escaping at the interpolation point reproduces each replayed reply's raw-path outcome, so any recovery under a disclosed boundary must come from the model changing its generation. Across eight same-window configurations, replaying the same reply through the added parser lowers success by 55.4 to 73.2 percentage points; disclosure recovers 30.4 to 60.7 points for six configurations, and zero or slightly negative for the other two. Raw generation is nearly saturated at the frontier; boundary adaptation is what still separates models. GPT-5.6-sol's matched gap of -3.6 points hides -64.3 points of damage and +60.7 points of compensation. The deployment configuration reorders models: one reversal among 26 comparable pairs is unambiguous and four more sit on single-task margins. Evaluations of command-issuing agents should report the model configuration, generation contract, execution path, operating point, and final-state validator rather than treat a matched score as an intrinsic model property.

</details>


### [176] [OmniScientist: An Omni-Modal Omni-Discipline AI Scientist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13558)
*Bobo Li,Hao Fei,Tianjie Ju,Mong-Li Lee,Wynne Hsu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 OmniScientist，一个端到端的多模态 AI 科研智能体系统，直接从异质原始证据（图像、信号、音频、视频、3D 结构、轨迹、表格、公式和图）开展跨学科研究。系统通过感知层和三个自主智能体（构思、实验、写作）在确定性流水线中运作，并在代码层面强制执行新颖性筛查、统计有效性、执行溯源和数值可追溯性。在 36 个真实数据案例、5 个学科家族上，系统全部完成从原始数据到论文手稿的完整流程，平均论文得分 6.3，直接感知优于仅接收预计算标量特征的盲变体约 85% 的成对比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 科学家系统虽然覆盖了从假设生成到手稿撰写的完整研究流程，但它们通常只能对文本、代码、标签或预计算摘要进行推理，无法获取对科学发现至关重要的空间、时间、跨通道和过程性关系。决策性证据对智能体不可用，限制了其科研能力。

Method: 提出 OmniScientist 系统，包含：(1) 感知层，用于从异质原始多模态证据中提取信息；(2) 三个自主智能体分别负责构思（ideation）、实验（experiment）和写作（writeup），在确定性流水线中运行，使观察结果贯穿影响研究问题、实验决策和最终结论；(3) 在代码层面执行构思、严谨性和主张检查，确保新颖性筛查、统计有效性、执行溯源和数值可追溯性。

Result: 在 36 个真实数据案例、5 个学科家族、4 类科学证据及多种模态上进行评估，系统在全部 36 个案例中完成了从原始数据到编译手稿的完整路径，参考推理骨干下平均论文得分 6.3。与仅接收预计算标量特征的盲变体相比，直接感知在全部 7 个评估维度上均有提升，并在 85% 的成对比较中获胜。

Conclusion: 全生命周期感知对于基于证据的科学发现至关重要。OmniScientist 证明了一种实用的路径，使 AI 科学家能够直接处理多模态原始证据，从而具备更广泛的科研能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in foundation models have enabled AI scientists to automate increasingly complete research workflows, from hypothesis generation and code execution to manuscript preparation. Yet workflow coverage alone does not provide access to the full evidence on which scientific discovery depends. Existing systems typically reason over text, code, labels, or precomputed summaries, leaving scientifically decisive spatial, temporal, cross-channel, and procedural relations unavailable to the agent. We introduce OmniScientist, an end-to-end, omni-modal AI scientist that conducts multidisciplinary research directly from heterogeneous raw evidence. A perception layer and 3 autonomous agents for ideation, experiment, and writeup operate within a deterministic pipeline, allowing observations to shape research questions, experimental decisions, and final claims throughout the research lifecycle. By running idea, rigour, and claim checks in code, the system enforces novelty screening, statistical validity, execution provenance, and numerical traceability. We evaluate OmniScientist on 36 real-data cases spanning 5 discipline families, 4 families of scientific evidence, and modalities including images, signals, audio, video, 3-D structures, trajectories, tables, formulae, and graphs. The system completes the full path from raw data to a compiled manuscript in all 36 cases and achieves a mean overall paper score of 6.3 with the reference reasoning backbone. In paired comparisons against a blind variant that receives only precomputed scalar features, direct perception improves all 7 evaluation dimensions and wins 85% of head-to-head judgments. These results show that lifecycle-wide perception is essential for evidence-grounded scientific discovery and provides a practical path toward broadly capable AI scientists.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [177] [RoboSynChallenge: Mastering Real-World Dexterity via Generalizing Synthesized Manipulation Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12416)
*Runyi Zhao,Ruixin Wu,Chengkun Li,Hongrui Zhang,Ang Li,Ruixing Jin,Yueci Deng,Yingying Guo,Lihe Ding,Shaocong Dong,Tianfan Xue,Yanjun Gao,Yudong Luo,Pascal Poupart,Simo Wu,Kui Jia,Wei-shi Zheng,Guiliang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboSynChallenge是一个统一基准测试竞赛，通过大规模合成数据生成与标准化真实世界机器人评估相结合，解决机器人操作泛化中真实数据稀缺的瓶颈。比赛提供多种基线策略（Transformer、Diffusion、VLA、World-Action-Model），最终仅在未见过的真实操作环境中评估，旨在促进数据高效、广泛可适应的操作系统的开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能中的机器人操作泛化仍是核心挑战，尽管模型架构和学习算法快速发展，但真实世界数据的稀缺和多样性不足严重制约了进展。现有基准测试缺乏统一性，难以跨任务、环境和难度级别系统评估操作策略的泛化能力。

Method: 该竞赛构建了一个统一基准，集成大规模合成数据生成与标准化真实世界评估。参与者可利用合成状态-动作轨迹提升通用策略学习，最终评估仅在未见过的真实操作环境中完成。提供包括Transformer、Diffusion、Vision-Language-Action和World-Action-Model在内的多种基线实现以确保可复现性和可比性。

Result: RoboSynChallenge通过将可扩展的仿真训练与严格真实世界验证相结合，提出了一个评估操作策略在广泛任务、环境和难度级别上泛化能力的统一基准框架，并提供了多样化的基线实现作为出发点。

Conclusion: RoboSynChallenge旨在通过合成数据与真实评估的耦合，推动开发能力强、数据高效且可适应的操作系统，为迈向真正通用的机器人智能铺平道路。

Abstract: Achieving generalizable robotic manipulation remains a central challenge in embodied intelligence. Despite rapid advances in model architectures and learning algorithms, progress is often limited by the scarcity and narrow diversity of real-world data. The RoboSynChallenge competition introduces a unified benchmark to evaluate and advance the generalizability of manipulation policies across a spectrum of tasks, environments, and difficulty levels. To alleviate the shortage of realistic data, the challenge integrates large-scale synthetic data generation with standardized real-world robotic evaluation. Participants are encouraged to leverage synthesized state-action trials to improve general-purpose policy learning, while final assessments are conducted exclusively on unseen real-world manipulation environments. Baseline implementations, including Transformer-, Diffusion-, Vision-Language-Action, and World-Action-Model-based policies, are provided to ensure reproducibility and comparability. By coupling scalable simulation-based training with rigorous real-world validation, RoboSynChallenge aims to foster the development of broadly capable, data-efficient, and adaptable manipulation systems, thereby paving the way toward truly general robotic intelligence.

</details>


### [178] [Attune: A Self-Annotation Tool for Understanding Robot Operator Attention Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12650)
*Puqi Zhou,Sungsoo Ray Hong,David Porfirio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 Attune 的部署前启发式工具，通过分析操作员眼动注视模式，为多机器人监督界面的机器人行为设计提供基于实证的注意力校准指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人监督中，操作员需同时监督多个机器人，而如何通过改变机器人行为来捕获、维持或释放操作员注意力缺乏实证指导。现有设计者缺乏关于"馈送内容"（即机器人行为设计）如何影响操作员注意力的经验性依据。

Method: 将操作员眼动注视视为机器人行为设计的线索，开发了部署前启发式工具 Attune。该工具能够自动识别有意义的注视转移时刻、提供 AI 辅助以标注注视转移原因，并输出操作员注视模式摘要供其审阅。通过用户研究让参与者标注吸引其注意力的视觉触发器来评估该工具。

Result: 研究发现操作员注视模式存在个体差异，Attune 工具能够有效帮助刻画和表征操作员注意力特征。

Conclusion: Attune 为设计者提供了校准机器人行为以适应不同操作员注意力特征的工具，填补了多机器人监督界面设计中关于机器人行为如何影响操作员注意力的实证空白。

Abstract: Deploying robot fleets in complex, real-world environments requires human operators to supervise multiple robots simultaneously. Managing operator attention is a fundamental challenge of designing multi-robot supervision interfaces, encompassing both feed layout and feed content (i.e., robot behavior design). Thus far, designers lack empirical guidance on the latter-how to change a robot's behavior to capture, sustain, or relinquish operator attention during multi-robot supervision. In our vision of the future, designers should be able to use this guidance to calibrate robot behavior to different operator attention profiles. Treating operator eye gaze as a robot behavior design clue, we created a pre-deployment elicitation tool called Attune. Attune automatically identifies when meaningful gaze shifts occur, provides AI assistance for annotating why shifts occurred, and outputs a summary of operator gaze patterns for operator review. We evaluated Attune through a user study in which participants annotated the visual triggers that drew their attention. Our findings unveil variation in observed gaze patterns and reveal how Attune helps characterize operator attention.

</details>


### [179] [FUSE: Active Functional Affordance Grounding through Adaptive Semantic-Geometric Evidence Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12683)
*Zhou Chen,Sathyanarayanan N. Aakur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出主动功能可供性接地（Active Functional Affordance Grounding）这一新任务，并设计FUSE框架，通过显式不确定性驱动的探索与学习式摊销规划器相结合，使智能体能在遮挡或不完整的功能线索下主动选择视角，实现对功能查询对象的空间定位。在Habitat基准上，FUSE取得了最高的非oracle接地性能，同时比全显式探索方法减少1.33倍计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体常需根据对象的功能而非外观身份来识别和交互，但现有可供性接地方法局限于固定视角，缺乏在功能线索被遮挡或不完整时主动决定"去哪里看"的机制，无法应对真实环境中需要的主动感知需求。

Method: 提出FUSE（自适应语义-几何证据获取框架），将显式不确定性驱动的探索与学习式摊销规划器相结合：显式探索负责量化当前观察的信息不确定性以识别信息量大的视角，摊销规划器则学习高效地选择观察路径，二者协同实现高效、低计算开销的主动视角选择。

Result: 在基于Habitat构建的基准上，FUSE取得了所有非oracle方法中最高的接地性能，同时相比完全显式探索方法将计算量降低1.33倍，并在多种可供性知识源上均保持有效性。

Conclusion: FUSE验证了将显式不确定性驱动探索与学习式摊销规划相结合的有效性，为具身智能体在功能导向的主动感知任务中提供了一个既高效又鲁棒的解决方案，且对不同的可供性知识源具有良好的泛化性。

Abstract: Embodied agents must often identify and interact with objects based on their function rather than their identity, requiring them to actively acquire observations that reveal discriminative functional evidence. Existing affordance grounding methods operate from fixed viewpoints and lack mechanisms for deciding where to look when functional cues are occluded or incomplete. We introduce Active Functional Affordance Grounding, a new task in which an agent sequentially explores a scene to identify and spatially ground an object satisfying a functional query. To address this problem, we propose FUSE, an adaptive semantic-geometric evidence acquisition framework that combines explicit uncertainty-driven exploration with a learned amortized planner to efficiently select informative viewpoints. We further introduce a Habitat-based benchmark for evaluating active functional grounding. Experiments show that FUSE achieves the highest observed non-oracle grounding performance while reducing computation by 1.33x relative to fully explicit exploration, and remains effective across multiple affordance knowledge sources.

</details>


### [180] [SAP-Nav: Spatial Semantic Representation Meets Active Perception for Hierarchical Open-Vocabulary Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12707)
*Xuetong Pei,Jian Liu,Vidura Munasinghe,Bo Miao,U-Xuan Tan,Wenrui Ding,Na Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAP-Nav是一个全在线、零样本的主动感知框架，通过增量构建可查询的空间语义表征（QSSR）和主动视角验证（AVV）来解决分层开放词汇目标导航（OVON）问题。在LangMap和HM3D-OVON基准上取得最佳综合性能，区域级导航SR相比训练方法提升12.2%，并已在真实机器人上验证可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层开放词汇目标导航（OVON）要求智能体在未知环境中依据自由形式指令（包含场景级、房间级、区域级和实例级提示）导航。尽管LangMap已形式化该任务，但在部分观测条件下可靠求解仍具挑战：空间定位需要持久的环境级证据，而目标验证需要清晰且具判别力的候选视图。现有方法难以同时满足这两个需求。

Method: SAP-Nav包含两个核心模块：(1) 增量构建可查询的空间语义表征（Queryable Spatial-Semantic Representation），从主动采集的房间视图中构建，支持从任意已探索位置进行空间语义查询；(2) 主动视角验证（Active Viewpoint Verification），评估当前观测是否提供充分证据，必要时重新定位智能体到信息量更大的视角，再依据类别和属性约束对候选目标进行验证。该方法为全在线、零样本，无需任务特定训练或预计算的场景地图。

Result: 在LangMap和HM3D-OVON基准上的实验表明，SAP-Nav取得整体最佳性能，其中在区域级导航上相比基于训练的方法SR提升12.2%。真实世界机器人实验进一步验证了其实用可行性。

Conclusion: SAP-Nav通过主动感知同时解决了空间定位和环境证据积累的问题，能够在分层OVON和标准类别级OVON上均取得优异表现，无需任务特定训练，展示了在真实机器人上部署的潜力。

Abstract: Hierarchical open-vocabulary object navigation (OVON) requires agents to follow free-form instructions that may specify targets through scene-, room-, region-, and instance-level cues in unseen environments. Although recent work LangMap has formalized this setting, reliably solving it under partial observations remains challenging: spatial grounding requires persistent environment-level evidence, whereas target verification requires clear and discriminative candidate views. We present SAP-Nav, a fully online, zero-shot framework that addresses both requirements through active perception. SAP-Nav incrementally constructs a Queryable Spatial-Semantic Representation from actively acquired room views, enabling spatial semantic queries from any explored location. It further employs Active Viewpoint Verification to assess whether the current observation provides sufficient evidence and, when necessary, reposition the agent to a more informative viewpoint before verifying candidates against category and attribute constraints. Although designed for hierarchical OVON, SAP-Nav supports both hierarchical and standard category-level OVON without task-specific training or precomputed scene maps. Experiments on LangMap and HM3D-OVON show that SAP-Nav achieves the overall best performance, including a 12.2% improvement in SR over training-based methods on region-level navigation. Real-world robot experiments further demonstrate its practical feasibility. Code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [181] [Genetic Fuzzy System-Based Multi-Robot Coordination for Planetary Missions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12755)
*Daegyun Choi,Donghoon Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于遗传模糊系统的多机器人系统（MRS）分散式协作搬运方法，在非结构化环境中最小化总路径长度并避开障碍物。通过高程图的坡度可穿越性分析将地图转换为二维可穿越图，利用遗传算法优化生成机器人速度的模糊推理系统（FIS），并在多种场景下进行训练和验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人协作搬运任务在非结构化环境中面临路径长度最小化和障碍物避让的双重挑战，现有集中式方法受限于通信和计算开销，需要一种分散式且鲁棒的解决方案。

Method: 1）基于高程图的坡度进行地形可穿越性分析，识别不可穿越区域并转化为障碍物，将地图降维为二维可穿越图；2）设计模糊推理系统（FISs）生成MRS搬运物体到目标位置的速度；3）使用遗传算法在多种场景（局部极小值、目标邻近障碍物、杂乱环境）中优化FIS参数。

Result: 训练后的FIS模型在转换后的可穿越图测试环境中通过多个场景进行了验证，结果表明该分散式方法能有效完成协作搬运任务。

Conclusion: 遗传模糊系统为多机器人协作搬运提供了一种有效的分散式方案，能够适应非结构化环境并实现路径优化，且无需中心化协调即可完成任务。

Abstract: This paper proposes a decentralized approach for a multi-robot system (MRS) using a genetic fuzzy system to perform a collaborative object transportation task that minimizes the total path length of the MRS in unstructured environment while avoiding obstacles. For an environment given by an elevation map, terrain traversability analysis with respect to the slope is performed to reduce the dimension and identify non-traversable areas that can be considered as obstacles, and the given map is converted into a traversability map in two dimensional space. In the training process, proposed fuzzy inference systems (FISs) to generate the MRS's velocity for transporting an object to a target position are optimized by a genetic algorithm with several scenarios, such as a local minima, a target that is close to an obstacle, and a cluttered environment. The trained FIS models are applied to the testing environment, which is the converted traversability map, and validated using multiple scenarios.

</details>


### [182] [AirForesight: Current-to-Future Spatial Map Imagination with Cross-Space Planning Consistency for UAV-VLN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12835)
*Yutong Liu,Xiaojie Li,Mingzhu Xu,Jianlong Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AirForesight框架，一种从当前到未来的空间地图想象机制，用于无人机视觉语言导航（UAV-VLN）任务。该框架通过多视角观测学习结构化当前地图表示，结合当前地图重建与未来轨迹预测的联合监督，利用结构化因果注意力将当前空间知识传播到未来地图推理，并通过跨空间规划一致性损失增强导航相关性，在OpenUAV和AerialVLN-S数据集上取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UAV-VLN方法大多直接将视觉语言输入映射到动作，缺乏显式的场景接地（scene grounding）和未来感知的空间推理能力，难以应对户外复杂环境中稀疏多视角观测带来的空间结构推断挑战。

Method: AirForesight框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 从多视角观测学习结构化当前地图表示，通过当前地图重建和未来轨迹预测联合监督；(2) 结构化因果注意力机制，将当前空间知识传播到未来地图推理；(3) 跨空间规划一致性损失，促使预测的地图空间轨迹与由真实航点位移推导的专家动作方向保持一致，最终聚合当前与未来表示预测下一个3D航点。

Result: 在OpenUAV和AerialVLN-S数据集上的实验以及大量消融研究验证了框架的强性能和稳定性。

Conclusion: AirForesight通过当前到未来的空间地图想象机制，为UAV-VLN提供了显式的场景接地和未来感知的空间推理能力，验证了结构化空间表征与因果推理在无人机导航中的有效性。

Abstract: Unmanned Aerial Vehicle Vision-Language Navigation (UAV-VLN) requires agents to follow language instructions, infer spatial structure from sparse multi-view observations, and execute feasible 3D motion in complex outdoor environments. Despite recent progress with large language models, most existing methods still map vision-language inputs directly to actions, providing limited explicit scene grounding and future-aware spatial reasoning. We propose AirForesight, a current-to-future spatial map imagination framework for UAV-VLN. AirForesight first learns a structured current-map representation from multi-view observations. This representation is jointly supervised by current-map reconstruction and future-trajectory prediction, encouraging it to encode both present scene structure and future motion intent. Under structured causal attention, the current spatial knowledge is propagated to future-map reasoning, and the resulting current and future representations are aggregated to predict the next 3D waypoint. To make spatial imagination more relevant to navigation, we introduce a cross-space planning consistency loss that encourages directional agreement between the predicted map-space trajectory and the expert action direction derived from the ground-truth waypoint displacement. Experiments on OpenUAV and AerialVLN-S, together with extensive ablations, demonstrate strong performance and support the effectiveness and stability of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [183] [ASPIRE-VINS: Adaptive Spline-based Visual-inertial Navigation System With Robust 3D Measurement Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12840)
*Kwangyik Jung,Eungchang Mason Lee,Taekjun Oh,Hyun Myung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ASPIRE-VINS，一种结合自适应节点放置（AKP）、多分辨率样条（MRS）和三维测量空间残差（3D-MSR）的连续时间视觉惯性导航系统（VINS），通过运动自适应节点分配和切空间局部细化，在不同运动和传感条件下取得优于或相当的轨迹精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统离散时间VINS方法（如IMU预积分）在任意时间戳残差评估或运动依赖的时间分辨率需求下灵活性不足；而连续时间样条方法虽以光滑时间函数表示轨迹，但均匀分布的节点在快速动态下欠表征、在静态区间过度参数化，缺乏运动自适应能力。

Method: ASPIRE-VINS包含三个核心组件：1）自适应节点放置（AKP），根据局部运动变化分配样条节点；2）多分辨率样条（MRS），在切空间中添加有界局部细化以增强拟合能力；3）三维测量空间残差（3D-MSR），通过将特征与校准观测射线在3D测量空间中对齐实现方位一致性约束。

Result: 实验表明ASPIRE-VINS在多种多样运动和感知条件下，轨迹误差相比基线方法具有竞争力或更低的误差，验证了运动自适应连续时间轨迹建模的有效性。

Conclusion: 运动自适应的连续时间轨迹表示能够有效解决离散方法灵活性不足和均匀节点样条参数化低效的问题，ASPIRE-VINS为多样运动场景下的VINS提供了一种鲁棒且精确的解决方案。

Abstract: Visual-inertial navigation systems estimate six-degree-of-freedom motion by fusing visual and inertial data. Modern discrete-time methods with IMU preintegration provide strong accuracy and efficiency, but keyframe-based representations can be less flexible when residuals must be evaluated at arbitrary timestamps or when motion-dependent temporal resolution is needed. Continuous-time splines address this issue by representing the trajectory as a smooth temporal function, but uniformly spaced knots can under-represent rapid dynamics or over-parameterize static intervals. This letter proposes ASPIRE-VINS, a continuous-time VINS framework that combines adaptive knot placement (AKP), multi-resolution splines (MRS), and 3D measurement-space residuals (3D-MSR). AKP allocates knots according to local motion variation, MRS adds bounded local refinement in tangent space, and 3D-MSR provides bearing consistency by aligning transformed features with calibrated observation rays in 3D measurement space. Experiments show that ASPIRE-VINS achieves competitive or lower trajectory errors than the compared baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of motion-adaptive continuous-time trajectory modeling under diverse motion and sensing conditions.

</details>


### [184] [BrainWAM: Action-Space Coordination of Semantic Priors and Predictive Dynamics for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12854)
*Bing Zhan,Shuyao Shang,Jiahao Gu,Shuo Lu,Yuan Xu,Zhao Wang,Yida Wang,Xueyang Zhang,Kun Zhan,Lue Fan,Zhaoxiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BrainWAM是一个为自动驾驶设计的结构化动作空间协调框架，将语义推理（VLA）与预测性世界建模（WAM）解耦为两条专门的动作导向通路，在紧凑动作表征层面进行对齐，并采用异步整流流推理策略降低延迟，在NAVSIM v1和v2上均达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需要在语义约束和预测性动力学两个层面进行规划，但现有的端到端方法往往只强调其中一方面：VLA模型利用VLM先验进行语义推理，WAM模型通过生成式世界建模提供未来感知预测。作者发现简单的token级注意力联合组合存在注意力分配不匹配问题，语义捷径会主导共享注意力空间并抑制预测性动力学，因此需要一个能同时利用两者优势的统一规划器。

Method: BrainWAM提出结构化动作空间协调框架，将语义推理和预测性世界建模转化为两条功能专门化的动作导向通路，并在紧凑动作表征级别对齐它们。此外引入异步整流流推理策略，通过解耦视频和动作的去噪过程来缩短推理延迟，同时保留与规划相关的预测上下文。

Result: BrainWAM在NAVSIM v1上达到89.5 PDMS，在NAVSIM v2上达到89.6 EPDMS，均达到当前最优水平，稳定超越仅使用VLA或仅使用WAM的方法。

Conclusion: BrainWAM通过借鉴神经科学中功能专门化系统协调产生复杂行为的原理，成功解决了语义先验与预测动力学融合时的注意力分配不匹配问题，证明了结构化动作空间协调是自动驾驶统一规划的一个实用且有前景的方向。

Abstract: Autonomous driving requires planning under both semantic constraints and predictive dynamics. Existing end-to-end driving approaches, however, typically emphasize only one side of this requirement: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models exploit VLM priors for semantic reasoning, while World Action Models (WAMs) provide future-aware prediction through generative world modeling. This naturally motivates a unified planner that can leverage both semantic priors and predictive dynamics. However, we find that a naive combination through joint token-level attention suffers from an attention-allocation mismatch, where semantic shortcuts dominate the shared attention space and suppress predictive dynamics. Inspired by neuroscience evidence that complex behavior arises from coordination among functionally specialized systems, we propose BrainWAM, a structured action-space coordination framework that converts semantic reasoning and predictive world modeling into two specialized action-oriented pathways, and aligns them at the level of compact action representations. We further introduce an asynchronous rectified-flow inference strategy with decoupled video and action denoising, which shortens inference latency while preserving planning-relevant predictive context. BrainWAM reaches state-of-the-art performance on both NAVSIM v1 (89.5 PDMS) and NAVSIM v2 (89.6 EPDMS), consistently outperforming VLA-only or WAM-only methods, highlighting BrainWAM as a practical and promising direction for autonomous driving systems.

</details>


### [185] [HumanoidVLN: A Physics-Grounded Simulator and Benchmark for Vision-Language Navigation Across Diverse Humanoid Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12860)
*Quan-Dung Pham,Anh Dao,The-Anh Nguyen,Minh Nguyen-Dinh,Phuong Nam Dang,Tri Pham,Hung Tran,Bach Dao,Tuyen P. Le,Truong Nguyen,Quan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HumanoidVLN，一个基于NVIDIA Isaac Sim的物理仿真器与基准，用于跨多种人形机器人形态的视觉语言导航（VLN）。平台支持四种人形机器人（Unitree G1/H1等），集成层次化控制栈（RL运动策略+PD/MPC路径跟踪器），并兼容NaVILA等四个VLN模型。通过双生成器+评审器+释义器多智能体管道生成933条碰撞感知参考轨迹，每条配有一条细粒度指令和三种风格变体。JanusVLN取得最高平均成功率43.55%。20次sim-to-real试点中导航误差相关性r=0.935。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN基准针对轮式智能体设计，未能覆盖人形机器人的双足运动物理约束、跨平台形态差异，以及运动诱发相机动态导致的自我中心观测失真。这些挑战需要物理接地的人形VLN仿真与基准来填补空白。

Method: 基于NVIDIA Isaac Sim构建HumanoidVLN仿真器，支持可扩展的人形配置（四种机器人，10-12自由度，1.17-1.80m身高），采用层次化控制栈；环境取自艺术设计场景与3D高斯泼溅重建，过滤可导航面积超100平方米的区域；指令经双生成器-评审器-释义器多智能体管道生成并有人类验证；集成四个VLN模型进行评测。

Result: 四个模型和四种形态中，JanusVLN取得最高平均成功率43.55%和nDTW 48.38。20次sim-to-real试点实验中，DualVLN在Unitree G1上导航误差与仿真强相关（r=0.935），平均绝对误差0.68m，平均轨迹相似度0.782 nDTW。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了VLN模型、控制器与人形形态在物理执行条件下的相互作用，为跨人形形态的VLN研究提供了物理接地的仿真与基准基础设施。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) for humanoid robots poses challenges existing benchmarks fail to address: bipedal locomotion imposes physical constraints absent from wheeled agents, humanoid morphologies vary across platforms, and egocentric observations are distorted by locomotion-induced camera dynamics. We present HumanoidVLN, a physics-grounded simulator and benchmark for VLN across diverse humanoid embodiments. Built on NVIDIA Isaac Sim, our platform supports an extensible set of humanoid configurations, demonstrated on four robots (Unitree G1, Unitree H1, Internal-A, Internal-B) spanning 10-12 lower-body DoF and heights from 1.17m to 1.80m, via a hierarchical control stack combining a reinforcement learning locomotion policy with interchangeable PD or MPC path trackers. New robots and VLN models integrate with minimal effort; we demonstrate compatibility with NaVILA, DualVLN, StreamVLN, and JanusVLN. Environments are drawn from artist-designed scenes and 3D Gaussian Splatting reconstructions, filtered for navigable areas exceeding 100 square meters. Instructions are generated by a dual generator-reviewer plus paraphraser multi-agent pipeline with human-in-the-loop verification, yielding 933 collision-aware reference episodes, each paired with one fine-grained instruction and three coarse-grained stylistic variants (formal, natural, casual). Across four models and four embodiments, JanusVLN achieves the highest mean success rate of 43.55% and nDTW of 48.38. In a 20-episode sim-to-real pilot with DualVLN and the Unitree G1, navigation errors correlate strongly (r=0.935), with a mean absolute difference of 0.68m and mean trajectory similarity of 0.782 (+/-0.188) nDTW. These results highlight the interaction between VLN models, controllers, and humanoid embodiments under physical execution. Code, benchmark, and data will be released upon acceptance at https://humanoid-vln.github.io/.

</details>


### [186] [AMR-Pose: An Active LED Marker-Based Relative Pose Estimation Framework With Probabilistic Switching PnP for Cooperative AUVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12866)
*Zeyu Sha,Xiaorui Wang,Mingyang Yang,Feitian Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AMR-Pose，一个基于主动LED标记的AUV（自主水下机器人）相对位姿估计框架。该框架采用一个中心红色LED加三个蓝色外围LED的紧凑标记模块，并结合概率切换PnP估计器（PSwPnP），融合SE(3)上的李群位姿传播、概率标记关联和可见性自适应测量融合，在复杂水下条件下实现鲁棒的六自由度相对位姿估计。水槽实验和闭环领航-跟随实验验证了其准确性和实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下环境中视觉相对定位面临严重挑战，包括水体浑浊、光照变化、反射和特征间歇性遮挡等光学退化问题，导致传统的视觉相对位姿估计方法在合作AUV之间难以实现稳健的相对定位，需要通过主动标记和鲁棒估计方法来解决。

Method: 提出AMR-Pose框架，包含两部分：(1) 紧凑标记模块，由中心红色LED和三个蓝色外围LED组成，安装在领航AUV上以提供特征显著的视觉标记；(2) 概率切换PnP估计器（PSwPnP），结合SE(3)上的李群位姿传播、概率标记关联和可见性自适应测量融合，能够根据标记可见性动态调整估计过程，在部分观测和可见性切换期间保持几何一致性和时间稳定性。

Result: 水槽实验中利用运动捕捉系统提供真值验证，AMR-Pose在挑战性水下条件下实现了准确、平滑且鲁棒的相对位姿估计。闭环领航-跟随实验进一步证明了其在实际合作水下机器人系统中实时相对位姿反馈的可行性。

Conclusion: AMR-Pose通过主动LED标记结合概率切换PnP估计器，有效解决了水下退化环境中的相对位姿估计问题，为合作AUV的多机器人协同提供了可靠的实时相对定位方案，具有实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Reliable relative pose estimation between autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) is critical for cooperative ocean exploration, sampling, and multi-robot coordination. However, achieving robust vision-based relative localization in underwater environments remains challenging due to severe optical degradation, including turbidity, illumination variations, reflections, and intermittent feature occlusions. This paper presents AMR-Pose, an active LED marker-based relative pose estimation framework for cooperative AUVs. A compact marker module consisting of one red central LED and three blue peripheral LEDs is developed and integrated onto the leader AUV to provide distinctive visual features under complex underwater conditions. Building upon the detected marker observations, a probabilistic switching Perspective-n-Point estimator (PSwPnP) is developed by combining Lie-group pose propagation on $SE(3)$, probabilistic marker association, and visibility-adaptive measurement fusion for robust six-degree-of-freedom relative pose estimation. The proposed framework dynamically adapts the estimation process according to marker visibility, maintaining geometric consistency and temporal stability during partial observations and visibility transitions. Extensive water-tank experiments with motion-capture ground truth validate that AMR-Pose achieves accurate, smooth, and robust relative pose estimation under challenging underwater conditions. Closed-loop leader-follower experiments further demonstrate its feasibility for real-time relative pose feedback in cooperative underwater robotics.

</details>


### [187] [Temporal GRPO: Beyond Trajectory-Level Credit in Vision-Language-Action Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13026)
*Yao Zhou,Hang Gao,Fengge Wu,Changwen Zheng,Wenwen Qiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Temporal GRPO，通过构建可检测的任务阶段、对齐阶段特定的动作区间，并仅比较进入同一阶段的rollout来计算阶段优势，解决VLA策略后训练中轨迹级信用混叠问题，在RoboTwin 2.0和LIBERO-Long上提升任务成功率与样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于GRPO的VLA后训练中，一个轨迹只产生一个rollout级优势并被应用于所有动作。如果一个rollout完成了多个有效阶段但在后期失败，早期产生进展的动作也会被惩罚，即轨迹级信用混叠（trajectory-level credit aliasing），导致信用分配不准确。

Method: Temporal GRPO包含三个关键步骤：1）构建可检测的任务阶段；2）将每个rollout与阶段特定的动作区间对齐；3）仅比较进入相同阶段的rollout，计算阶段级优势并应用于对应动作区间，在单次策略更新中完成。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0上，Temporal GRPO提升了任务成功率和样本效率，在不同任务horizon上表现一致。在LIBERO-Long上的受控更新中，该方法保留了共享的先决阶段，并将改进集中在rollout结果首次出现分歧的第一个阶段。

Conclusion: 通过阶段级别的信用分配替代轨迹级别的全局优势，Temporal GRPO有效缓解了信用混叠问题，使VLA策略的后训练更具精确性和样本效率。

Abstract: Outcome-driven reinforcement learning offers a scalable way to post-train vision-language-action (VLA) policies from sparse task-success feedback. In common GRPO-based VLA post-training, one rollout-level advantage is applied to every action in the trajectory. A rollout that completes several valid stages but fails later can therefore penalize the actions that produced its earlier progress. We call this trajectory-level credit aliasing. Temporal GRPO addresses this problem by constructing detectable task stages, aligning each rollout with stage-specific action intervals, and comparing only rollouts that have entered the same stage. The resulting stage advantages are applied to their corresponding intervals in a single policy update. On RoboTwin 2.0, Temporal GRPO improves task success and sample efficiency, with consistent gains across task horizons. Controlled updates on LIBERO-Long preserve shared prerequisite stages and concentrate improvement at the first stage where rollout outcomes diverge.

</details>


### [188] [H2R-Bench: Benchmarking Human-to-Robot Manipulation Video Generation in World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13049)
*Dingyi Rong,Yue Shi,Chaofan Ma,Jiezhang Cao,Zongrui Wang,Zeyu Zhang,Yao Mu,Guangtao Zhai,Ning Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出H2R-Bench基准测试，用于评估从人类第一视角演示视频到机器人操作视频的跨实体视频生成质量，涵盖两个机器人实体和六个操作类别，评估了十一个视频生成模型，发现现有视频世界模型在人到机器人操作迁移中仍存在实体一致性、功能交互和任务执行方面的显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模机器人操作数据是机器人学习的关键，但机器人演示收集昂贵且难以规模化；人体第一视角操作视频丰富但跨实体迁移存在人手与机器人末端执行器差异的挑战。视频世界模型有望从人类观察中合成机器人视角操作视频，但其跨实体迁移能力尚未被系统探索。

Method: 构建H2R-Bench基准，将人体第一视角演示视频在指定实体约束下转化为机器人操作视频。每个基准实例包含人类演示视频、目标实体约束和源地面标注（任务目标、动作事件、功能接触、物体响应）。从目标状态完成度、动作事件完成度、功能接触迁移、实体正确性和通用视频质量五个维度评估生成视频。对六个操作类别和两个机器人实体下的十一个视频生成模型进行基准测试。

Result: 评估显示当前视频世界模型在人到机器人操作迁移方面仍存在明显局限：即使是领先模型也常在实体一致性、功能交互和任务执行方面失败。

Conclusion: H2R-Bench为评估视频世界模型能否弥合人到机器人实体差距、将人类操作观察转化为机器人训练资源提供了系统化诊断框架，并揭示了现有模型的不足与发展方向。

Abstract: Large-scale manipulation data is essential for robot learning, yet collecting robot demonstrations remains expensive and difficult to scale. Meanwhile, abundant egocentric human manipulation videos provide rich behavioral experiences, but transferring them across embodiments remains challenging due to differences between human hands and robotic end-effectors. Recent advances in video world models offer a promising pathway to synthesize robot-centric manipulation videos from human observations, while their cross-embodiment transfer capability remains largely unexplored. Therefore, we introduce H2R-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating cross-embodiment human-to-robot manipulation video generation, where models transform egocentric human demonstrations into robot manipulation videos under specified embodiments. Each benchmark instance contains a human demonstration video, target embodiment constraints, and source-grounded annotations covering task goals, action events, functional contacts, and object responses. H2R-Bench evaluates generated videos through five dimensions, including goal-state completion, action-event completion, functional contact transfer, embodiment correctness, and general video quality. We benchmark eleven state-of-the-art video generation models across six manipulation families and two robot embodiments. Our evaluation reveals that current video world models remain limited in human-to-robot manipulation transfer: even leading models often fail in embodiment consistency, functional interaction, and task execution. H2R-Bench provides a systematic diagnostic framework for evaluating whether video world models can bridge the human-to-robot embodiment gap and convert human manipulation observations into robot-centric training resources.

</details>


### [189] [Semantic Radiance Fields as Simulators for Spatial Reasoning in Real-World Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13095)
*Nico Heider,Michał Jan Włodarczyk,Katarzyna Wasielewska-Michniewska,Przemysław Hołda,Martin Schieck,Marcin Paprzycki,Maria Ganzha,Bogdan Franczyk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出利用语义辐射场（Semantic Radiance Fields, SRF）作为具身智能体空间推理的仿真器，将预训练视觉模型的2D语义分割提升为3D辐射场，统一编码几何、外观和逐类语义身份，从而在不牺牲真实感的前提下提供ground truth语义标注，支持新视角合成、语义查询和自由空间查询，并示例展示了果园苹果抓取任务的SRF驱动仿真器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练和评估具身智能体的空间推理能力需要既几何保真又语义可查询的多样化环境。合成仿真器提供ground truth语义但缺乏真实感；基于真实场景重建的仿真器外观真实但默认缺乏ground truth语义。现有方法无法同时满足这两项需求，亟需一种能兼顾两者的表示与仿真方案。

Method: 提出使用语义辐射场（SRF）作为仿真器：从带位姿的RGB图像出发，利用预训练视觉模型生成2D语义分割，并将其提升到3D辐射场中，联合编码几何、外观和逐类别语义身份。该表示支持新视角合成、语义查询和自由空间查询，并可与物理引擎集成。作为示例应用，构建了果园苹果抓取任务的SRF驱动仿真器，由辐射场为物理引擎提供相机渲染、语义ground truth和占据查询。

Result: 该方法能够从真实场景的RGB采集高效生成多样化的真实世界环境，用于训练和评估空间推理模型，并成功展示了在果园苹果抓取任务中的实际应用。

Conclusion: SRF作为一种统一的场景表示，填补了合成仿真与真实场景重建仿真之间的空白，在单个接地表示中同时满足几何保真、语义可查询和真实外观的需求，为空间推理智能体的训练与评测提供了新范式。

Abstract: Training and evaluating spatial reasoning in embodied agents requires diverse environments that are both geometrically faithful and semantically queryable. Synthetic simulators offer ground truth semantics but sacrifice realism; simulators based on reconstructions of real-world environments have realistic appearance but lack ground truth semantics by default. We propose using Semantic Radiance Fields (SRF) as simulators for spatial reasoning agents. SRFs are a representation that unifies these requirements by lifting 2D semantic segmentations from pretrained vision models into a 3D radiance field that jointly encodes geometry, appearance, and per-class semantic identity. The resulting fields are reconstructed from posed RGB captures of real scenes and support novel-view synthesis, semantic and free-space queries within a single grounded representation. This enables the efficient generation of diverse real-world environments to train and evaluate spatial reasoning models. As an example application, we outline an SRF-driven simulator for an orchard apple-reaching task, in which the radiance field supplies camera rendering, semantic ground truth, and occupancy queries to a physics engine.

</details>


### [190] [S2-HWM: Sparse Event-Structured Hierarchical World Model for Long-Horizon Surgical Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13103)
*Shuzhe Zhang,Xin Zhu,Yinling Qian,Qiong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出S2-HWM，一种稀疏事件结构化的分层世界模型，通过从原始潜在轨迹中学习稀疏事件证据来协调事件级管理器和原始步工人，在PegTransfer任务上实现98.7%成功率，比GAS DreamerV3基线高出22.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程手术机器人操作面临奖励稀疏和交互变化不规则的问题。现有世界模型以原始步分辨率进行想象，无法捕捉可变时长任务进展；手动指定阶段虽能提供中间结构，但其任务特定边界难以与状态依赖的交互转换对齐。

Method: S2-HWM从原始潜在轨迹中学习稀疏事件证据，用于调度事件级管理器的目标更新，同时每个选定的潜在目标条件化工人的原始动作直到下一次更新。学习到的事件证据还构成可变时长段用于事件转换模型（ETM），该模型预测下一边界随机状态、段持续时间和累积段奖励，通过链式事件级预测提供超出原始想象范围的可变时长延续。

Result: 在基于SurRoL的PegTransfer任务上，S2-HWM成功率达到98.7%，比平坦的GAS DreamerV3基线高出22.7个百分点。

Conclusion: S2-HWM通过自动学习事件证据而非手动指定阶段，有效地将分层世界模型应用于稀疏奖励的长时程手术操作任务，验证了事件级结构化想象相对于平坦想象范式的显著优势。

Abstract: Long-horizon surgical robot manipulation is challenging because task rewards are sparse, while meaningful interaction changes occur at irregular intervals. Existing world-model agents typically imagine at primitive-step resolution, leaving variable-duration task progress implicit. Manually specified stages can provide intermediate structure, but their task specific boundaries are difficult to align with state-dependent interaction transitions. We propose S2-HWM, a Sparse Event-Structured Hierarchical World Model that learns sparse event evidence from primitive latent trajectories to coordinate an event-level manager and a primitive-step worker. The event evidence schedules manager goal updates, and each selected latent goal conditions the worker's primitive actions until the next update. The learned event evidence also forms variable-duration segments for an Event Transition Model (ETM), which predicts the next?boundary stochastic state, segment duration, and accumulated segment reward. Chaining these event-level predictions provides a variable-duration continuation beyond the primitive imagination horizon for manager learning, while the worker retains primitive-step actor-critic learning. On a SurRoL-based PegTransfer task, S2-HWM achieves a success rate of 98.7%, outperforming the flat GAS DreamerV3 baseline by 22.7 percentage points.

</details>


### [191] [FAM-DQ: A Dual-Quadrotor-Based Fully Actuated Aerial Manipulator for High-Torque Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13220)
*Xuwei Yang,Ruoyu Ren,Ziqian Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FAM-DQ，一种基于双四旋翼的全驱动空中机械臂。通过在中央框架两端安装两个四旋翼推进模块（通过被动关节连接），并利用齿轮驱动舵机调节指向方向，FAM-DQ实现了6自由度解耦末端执行器控制，具备全向操作能力和增强的扭矩输出。实验验证了其轨迹跟踪、姿态跟踪、静态扭矩测量和拧螺丝等能力，最大扭矩达1.019 N·m，总质量0.447 kg，扭矩质量比2.28 N·m/kg。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统欠驱动空中机械臂存在强位置-姿态耦合问题，而全驱动平台设计往往面临结构复杂、载荷能力有限和扭矩输出不足等缺点。需要一种既能产生大交互力/力矩又能保持精确末端控制、同时结构简单且扭矩输出强的空中操作平台。

Method: FAM-DQ采用双四旋翼结构：两个四旋翼推进模块通过被动关节安装于中央框架两端，齿轮驱动舵机用于调节指向方向。该构型实现6自由度解耦控制，提供全向操作能力和增强的扭矩输出，同时保持总质量仅为0.447 kg的轻量化设计。

Result: 实验涵盖轨迹跟踪、姿态跟踪、静态扭矩测量及拧螺丝操作。FAM-DQ达到最大扭矩1.019 N·m，总质量0.447 kg，扭矩质量比为2.28 N·m/kg。

Conclusion: FAM-DQ通过创新的双四旋翼构型有效解决了全驱动空中机械臂在结构复杂度与扭矩输出之间的权衡，以轻量化设计实现了高扭矩物理交互能力，验证了其在空中物理交互任务中的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Aerial physical interaction requires aerial manipulation platforms to generate large interaction forces and torques while maintaining precise end-effector control. However, conventional underactuated aerial manipulators suffer from strong position-attitude coupling, whereas fully actuated platform designs often face structural complexity, limited payload capacity, and insufficient torque output. This paper presents FAM-DQ, a dual-quadrotor based fully actuated aerial manipulator designed for high-torque physical interaction tasks. By mounting two quadrotor propulsion modules at the ends of a central frame through passive joints, while using a gear-driven servo to regulate the pointing direction, FAM-DQ achieves decoupled $6$-DoF end-effector control with omnidirectional manipulation capability and enhanced torque output. Experiments including trajectory tracking, attitude tracking, static torque measurement, and screw driving validate the proposed design. FAM-DQ achieves a maximum torque of $1.019~\mathrm{N}\cdot\mathrm{m}$ with a total mass of $0.447~\mathrm{kg}$, corresponding to a torque-to-mass ratio of $2.28~\mathrm{N}\cdot\mathrm{m/kg}$.

</details>


### [192] [Manufacturing Complex Airtight Soft Pneumatic Actuators for Soft Robotics: Process Evaluation and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13233)
*Mohammed Abboodi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对复杂软体气动执行器的多种制造工艺进行了系统性评估，包括热缩成型、硅胶浇筑、粉末和液体基增材制造以及FDM。研究发现FDM是最具适应性的制造路线，因其主要缺陷可通过工艺优化逐步消除，且气密性不仅取决于壁厚还取决于挤出路径结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制造复杂软体气动执行器极具挑战性，因为几何保真度、柔顺性、结构完整性、气密性需要同时满足。现有的制造工艺缺乏系统比较，难以区分工艺固有局限与可修正的制造缺陷。

Method: 研究采用制造导向的评估方法，对多种工艺路线进行过程筛选、基线制造、失效分析和工艺改进四个环节，以区分工艺固有局限与可修正缺陷。评估的工艺包括热缩成型、硅胶浇筑、粉末基和液体基增材制造（含DLP）以及FDM。

Result: 热缩成型受限于几何一致性；浇筑受限于模具可达性和粘接界面；粉末基方法因封闭通道内残留材料而受限；DLP受限于材料特性和后处理要求；FDM是最具适应性的路线，其主导缺陷可通过工艺优化逐步消除。研究还发现气密性不仅依赖名义壁厚，还取决于挤出路径架构，支持无支撑几何结构对内部后处理受限时至关重要。

Conclusion: 研究建立了面向制造的设计方法，工艺选择应以执行器架构与制造约束之间的兼容性为指导，为开发复杂、柔顺、气密的软体气动执行器提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Manufacturing complex soft pneumatic actuators remains challenging because geometric fidelity, compliance, structural integrity, and airtightness must be achieved simultaneously. This study presents a manufacturing-focused evaluation of several fabrication routes for complex pneumatic structures, including heat-shrink forming, silicone casting, powder- and liquid-based additive manufacturing, and fused deposition modeling (FDM). The processes were assessed through process screening, baseline fabrication, failure analysis, and process improvement to distinguish inherent process limitations from correctable manufacturing defects. Heat-shrink forming was limited by geometric conformity, casting by mold accessibility and bonded interfaces, powder-based methods by residual material trapped within enclosed passages, and digital light processing by the material properties and post-processing requirements of the investigated system. FDM provided the most adaptable route because its dominant defects could be progressively reduced through process optimization. The results further showed that airtightness depends not only on nominal wall thickness but also on extrusion-path architecture, while support-free geometry is important when access for internal post-processing is limited. These findings establish a practical design-for-manufacturing approach in which process selection is guided by the compatibility between actuator architecture and manufacturing constraints. The proposed approach provides practical guidance for developing complex, flexible, and airtight soft pneumatic actuators for soft robotic applications

</details>


### [193] [Predictive Relative-Velocity Steering for Safe Robotic Manipulator Teleoperation in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13284)
*Changhao Hu,Zeyi Liu,Songqiao Hu,Shuang Liu,Zihan Meng,Xiao He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级、模块化的主动碰撞避免框架，直接在末端执行器速度指令层面工作。该框架通过预测碰撞时间（TTC）并利用罗德里格斯旋转公式偏转相对速度向量来实现碰撞规避，同时避免了传统人工势场法的死锁问题。仿真与实物实验均验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥操作机器人虽能执行类人手臂的灵巧运动，但操作员在遇到突然出现的障碍物时，尤其在网络延迟或注意力受限的情况下，可能无法及时有效地避障，造成安全隐患。现有方法缺乏在速度指令层面快速响应的方案。

Method: 该框架包含三个核心部分：(1) 预处理点云数据；(2) 基于碰撞时间（TTC）预测潜在碰撞，并集成超调保护机制，同时补偿遥操作管线中点云处理的延迟；(3) 使用罗德里格斯旋转公式偏转相对速度向量，仅改变方向而保持速度大小不变，从而规避传统人工势场法（APF）的死锁问题。轻量化设计支持遥操作所需的高频控制。

Result: 在多种场景的仿真中，该方法相比基线方法获得了更高的末端执行器碰撞避免成功率；在真实物理机器人系统上的实验进一步验证了其碰撞避免的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过在速度指令层面进行主动碰撞避免，有效解决了遥操作系统在延迟和注意力受限条件下的突发性避障难题，且兼顾实时性与鲁棒性，为遥操作安全提供了可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in teleoperation have enabled robotic manipulators to perform dexterous, human-arm-like motions. However, human operators may fail to avoid suddenly appearing obstacles promptly and effectively, particularly under network latency or limited attention, thereby creating safety risks. To address this issue, we propose a lightweight and modular framework for proactive collision avoidance, operating directly at the end-effector velocity-command level. After preprocessing the point cloud, the framework first predicts potential collisions based on time-to-collision (TTC) with integrated overshoot protection, and subsequently rotates the relative-velocity vector using Rodrigues' rotation formula. The deflection changes only the direction of the relative velocity while preserving its magnitude, thereby mitigating the deadlock problem commonly encountered by conventional artificial potential field (APF) methods. The prediction module compensates for point-cloud processing latency introduced by complex teleoperation pipelines, while the lightweight design enables the high-frequency control required for teleoperation. Simulations across diverse scenarios show that the proposed method achieves a higher end-effector collision avoidance rate than the baseline methods. Experiments on a physical robotic system further validate its collision-avoidance effectiveness.

</details>


### [194] [NestDex: Nested Policy Learning with Copilot Assisted Teleoperation for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13362)
*James Zhao,Jinhe Tang,Mingyuan Ba,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NestDex是一个嵌套策略学习框架，通过使用学习到的手部技能辅助演示收集，减少灵巧操作中完整任务演示的采集负担。操作者仅需控制手臂并通过单自由度离合器调节活动的手部技能，内层手部策略根据本体感受历史自适应调整动作，视觉-语言选择器为每个任务阶段激活合适技能。生成的演示用于训练独立的外层视觉运动策略，部署时无需内层策略即可同时控制手臂和手部。真实世界实验表明NestDex提升了演示可靠性和效率，并支持有效的自主策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作能为机器人带来更丰富的物理世界交互，但学习这些行为受限于收集一致、完整任务演示的困难。与平行夹爪操作不同，灵巧任务要求操作者在整个任务过程中协调手臂运动与精细、接触密集的手指行为，这显著增加了演示收集的难度和负担。现有方法无法有效解决这一瓶颈，亟需减少操作者负担的演示收集范式。

Method: 提出NestDex嵌套策略学习框架：操作者控制手臂并通过单自由度离合器调节活动的手部技能，而非直接指定完整手指轨迹；内层手部策略从最新本体感受历史中自适应调整动作，视觉-语言选择器负责为任务各阶段激活合适技能；随后生成的演示用于训练独立的外层视觉运动策略，部署时无需内层策略即可同时控制手臂和手部；采用手部动作变分自编码器提供紧凑的手部动作目标，同时保留关节空间的手臂指令。

Result: 在真实世界的灵巧操作实验中，NestDex提升了演示收集的可靠性和效率，经验评估支持有效的自主策略学习。

Conclusion: NestDex通过嵌套策略框架显著降低了灵巧操作演示收集的负担，证明了一种更高效、更可靠的灵巧操作示范收集与自主策略学习范式。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation promises substantially richer robot interaction with the physical world, but learning these behaviours remains constrained by the difficulty of collecting consistent, complete-task demonstrations. Unlike parallel-jaw manipulation, dexterous tasks require the operator to coordinate arm motion with precise, contact-rich finger behaviour throughout the task. We introduce NestDex, a nested policy-learning framework that reduces this burden by using learned hand skills to assist demonstration collection. The operator controls the arm and regulates the active hand skill through a single-DoF clutch, rather than directly specifying the full finger trajectory. The inner hand policy adapts its motion from the latest proprioceptive history, while a vision-language selector activates the appropriate skill for each task stage. The resulting demonstrations train a separate outer visuomotor policy that controls both the arm and hand without the inner policies at deployment. A hand-action variational autoencoder provides compact hand-action targets while retaining arm commands in joint space. Across real-world dexterous manipulation experiments, NestDex improves demonstration reliability and efficiency, and the resulting empirical evaluations support effective autonomous policy learning. Video Demo are available at project website https://aus.bot/research/nestdex.

</details>


### [195] [FIRE-VLA: Failure-Informed Self-Evolution for Vision-Language-Action Models in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13395)
*Hao Dou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了FIRE-VLA，一种失败感知的自进化框架，通过从冻结的轮次初始模型副本进行自蒸馏，将强化学习中外部分组（低奖励、低多样性）中未解决的失败转化为特权监督信号，改进自动驾驶VLA规划模型的轨迹质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO在分组相对策略优化中，当所有采样轨迹都较差时，只能对失败进行排序而无法识别超出失败区域的行为，导致策略难以从失败中学习。

Method: FIRE-VLA在低奖励、低多样性分组中触发从冻结轮次初始模型副本的自蒸馏。师生模型参数规模相同，但仅教师能看到隐藏的未来轨迹。监督遵循学生生成的前缀并限制在回答token上，同时对每个分组保持GRPO活跃。更新后的策略成为下一轮的教师，使失败分布随策略演化。

Result: 在6019个样本和150个nuScenes场景上，FIRE-VLA保持可比较的单样本规划性能，G=4平均L2误差从1.848降至1.500米，评估持续失败率从13.03%降至11.20%。误差降低主要来自罕见的严重失败轨迹而非普通轨迹的均匀改善。

Conclusion: 通过将强化学习未能解决的失败案例转化为特权自监督信号，FIRE-VLA无需更大的外部教师即可有效降低自动驾驶VLA的严重失败轨迹误差，且策略自进化使失败分布得以动态调整。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning improves autonomous-driving vision-language-action (VLA) models by evaluating trajectories sampled from the current policy. Group relative policy optimization (GRPO) learns from reward differences within each rollout group. When all sampled trajectories are poor, this relative signal can rank failures without identifying behavior outside the failed region. We introduce FIRE-VLA, a failure-informed self-evolution framework that converts such unresolved failures into privileged supervision for the next policy. Low-reward, low-diversity groups trigger self-distillation from a frozen round-start copy of the same model. Teacher and student have the same parameter scale, but only the teacher observes the hidden future trajectory. Supervision follows the student's generated prefix and is restricted to answer tokens, while GRPO remains active for every group. The updated policy supplies the teacher for the next round, allowing the routed failure distribution to change with the policy without requiring a larger external teacher. Starting from the same Qwen2.5-VL-3B SFT checkpoint, the comparison matches student rollout and policy-update counts. On 6,019 examples from 150 held-out nuScenes scenes, FIRE-VLA retains comparable single-sample planning, reduces G=4 mean L2 from 1.848 to 1.500 m, and lowers evaluation-persistent failure prevalence from 13.03% to 11.20%. The reduction in mean error arises mainly from rare severe rollouts rather than uniform improvement across ordinary trajectories.

</details>


### [196] [Capstan-driven Continuum Surgical Robot: Design, Modeling, and Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13396)
*Gang Zhang,Yufu Qiu,Junyan Yan,Wenhui Zeng,Wenlong Lu,Shing Shin Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于"致动-感知协同设计"理念的集成设计-建模-感知框架，通过在电机安装支架中引入柔性元件实现实时缆绳张力测量（避免占用紧凑的绞盘空间），并基于多体短厚梁模型的并行计算框架捕获剪切效应与多缆协同交互，结合近端多轴力/力矩传感器实现单段和双段配置的连续型手术机器人的实时形状与力感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑型绞盘驱动连续型手术机器人长期面临形状与力感知的关键瓶颈，主要困难在于在受限的绞盘组件内难以获取缆绳张力信息。

Method: 1）致动-感知协同设计：在驱动系统电机安装支架引入柔性元件，使其在缆绳反作用力下产生微形变，从而在不占用紧凑绞盘空间的情况下实现实时缆绳张力测量；2）基于多体短厚梁模型的并行计算框架：解决空间缆绳布线策略带来的非常规关节构型建模复杂性，捕获短梁段剪切效应和多缆协同交互，同时实现实时性能；3）将近端多轴力/力矩传感器作为附加测量锚点，实现稳定的形状与力感知。

Result: 在单段和双段连续型机器人上均验证了所提框架的有效性，实现了实时末端姿态估计以及接触力和位置感知。

Conclusion: 通过在不影响紧凑绞盘架构的前提下提供缆绳张力反馈，该框架使绞盘驱动连续型手术机器人的集成感知成为可能。

Abstract: Shape and force sensing have long been critical bottlenecks in the development of compact capstan-driven continuum surgical robots, primarily due to the difficulty of obtaining cable tension information within the confined capstan assembly. To overcome these challenges, this paper presents an integrated design-modeling-sensing approach based on the concept of actuation-perception co-design. A compliant element is introduced into the motor mounting bracket of the drive system, enabling micro-deformation under the cable reaction force and thereby allowing real-time cable tension measurement without occupying the compact capstan space. To address the modeling complexity arising from unconventional joint configurations introduced by the spatial cable routing strategy, a parallel computation framework based on a multibody short-thick-beam model is proposed, which captures shear effects in short beam segments and synergistic multi-cable interactions while achieving real-time performance. Building on this framework, stable shape and force sensing is achieved by incorporating a proximal multi-axis force/torque sensor as an additional measurement anchor. Following this design-modeling-sensing framework, capstan-driven continuum surgical robots with single- and dual-segment configurations are developed. Experimental results validate the proposed framework in both single- and dual-segment continuum robots, demonstrating real-time tip pose estimation together with contact force and location perception. By enabling cable tension feedback without compromising the compact capstan architecture, the proposed framework makes integrated perception feasible for capstan-driven continuum surgical robots.

</details>


### [197] [Deliberate Practice: Learning Robot Skills under a Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13415)
*Shivam Vats,Sudarshan Harithas,Mete Tuluhan Akbulut,Arvind Raghunathan,George Konidaris*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为"Deliberate Practice (DP)"的主动技能学习算法，在有限的练习预算下为顺序任务计算预算最优的技能练习分配方案，通过双线性规划精确求解，使机器人最大化期望累积奖励并提升长时域操作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限的练习预算下，自主学习顺序任务的机器人技能是一个挑战性问题，需要在组合爆炸的技能方案空间中合理分配练习时间，以最大化任务的累积奖励。

Method: 提出Deliberate Practice算法，同时估计掌握技能所需的时间以及技能所解锁任务规划的累积奖励，将预算最优分配问题建模为双线性规划（bilinear program），可利用现成求解器精确求解。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界的长时域操作任务实验中，该方法使机器人能够最优地利用有限的练习时间来获取有用的策略，并改善长时域规划性能。

Conclusion: DP算法提供了可证明的预算最优技能练习分配方案，有效解决了有限预算下顺序任务机器人技能学习的组合优化难题，在长时域操作任务中表现出良好的实用性与有效性。

Abstract: We consider the problem of autonomously learning robot skills under a limited practice budget for sequential tasks. We propose an active skill learning algorithm, \emph{Deliberate Practice (DP)}, that computes a provably \emph{budget-optimal} allocation---practicing skills that maximize expected cumulative reward while being learnable within the budget. DP estimates both the time needed to master skills and the cumulative reward of the task plans that the skills unlock. Computing a budget-optimal allocation is challenging as it requires reasoning about combinatorially many skill plans over a large practice budget. Our key contribution is a bilinear program that can compute this exactly using off-the-shelf solvers. Through simulated and real-world experiments on long-horizon manipulation tasks, we show that our approach allows robots to optimally use limited practice time to acquire useful policies and improve long-horizon planning.

</details>


### [198] [Attention from Action, for Action: Emergent Visual Bottlenecks for Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13422)
